import { logger } from "hono/logger";
import { secureHeaders } from "hono/secure-headers";

const health = (c: Context) => c.json({ status: "ok" });

  .use(logger())
  .use(secureHeaders())
  .get("/api/health", health);

import { Hono } from "hono";
import type { Context } from "hono";
import { logger } from "hono/logger";
import { secureHeaders } from "hono/secure-headers";

import { auth, db } from "#src/context.ts";

const authHandler = async (c: Context) => await auth.handler(c.req.raw);

const health = async (c: Context) => {
  await db.connection.getClient().db().command({ ping: 1 });

  return c.json({ status: "ok" });
};

export const app = new Hono()
  .use(logger())
  .use(secureHeaders())
  .on(["GET", "POST"], "/api/auth/*", authHandler)
  .get("/api/health", health);

import type { Context } from "hono";
import { logger } from "hono/logger";
import { secureHeaders } from "hono/secure-headers";

  await db.$queryRaw`select 1`;

  return c.json({ status: "ok" });
};

export const app = new Hono()
  .use(logger())
  .use(secureHeaders())
  .on(["GET", "POST"], "/api/auth/*", authHandler)
  .get("/api/health", health);

import { Hono } from "hono";
import type { Context } from "hono";
import { logger } from "hono/logger";
import { secureHeaders } from "hono/secure-headers";

import { db } from "#src/context.ts";

const health = async (c: Context) => {
  await db.$queryRaw`select 1`;

  return c.json({ status: "ok" });
};

export const app = new Hono()
  .use(logger())
  .use(secureHeaders())
  .get("/api/health", health);

#   tanstack-start-hono-openapi-sqlite-better-auth-expo-docker
#   tanstack-start-hono-openapi-sqlite-better-auth-expo-docker-e2e
import { Hono } from "hono";
import type { Context } from "hono";
import { logger } from "hono/logger";
import { secureHeaders } from "hono/secure-headers";

import { createApi } from "@my-app/api";

import { auth, db } from "#src/context.ts";

const authHandler = async (c: Context) => await auth.handler(c.req.raw);

export const app = new Hono()
  .use(logger())
  .use(secureHeaders())
  .on(["GET", "POST"], "/api/auth/*", authHandler)
  .route("/api", createApi({ auth, db }));

import { logger } from "hono/logger";
import { secureHeaders } from "hono/secure-headers";

import { openApi, rpc } from "#src/orpc.ts";

export const app = new Hono()
  .use(logger())
  .use(secureHeaders())
  .all("/rpc/*", rpc)
  .all("/api/*", openApi);

import { logger } from "hono/logger";
import { secureHeaders } from "hono/secure-headers";

import { createAuth } from "@my-app/auth";
import { createDb } from "@my-app/db";

import { env } from "#src/env.ts";

export const db = createDb(env.DATABASE_URL);

export const auth = createAuth({
  baseURL: env.BETTER_AUTH_URL,
  db,
  secret: env.BETTER_AUTH_SECRET,
});

#   spa-hono-orpc-mysql-better-auth-void-e2e
import { env as bindings, waitUntil } from "cloudflare:workers";
import { env } from "void/env";

import { createAuth } from "@my-app/auth";
import type { Auth } from "@my-app/auth";
import { createDb } from "@my-app/db";
import type { Database } from "@my-app/db";

export interface Services {
  auth: Auth;
  db: Database;
}

export const withServices = async (
  handle: (services: Services) => Promise<Response>
) => {
  const db = createDb(env.DATABASE_URL ?? bindings.HYPERDRIVE.connectionString);
  let streaming = false;

  const dispose = async () => {
    await db.$client.promise().end();
  };

  try {
    const services = {
      auth: createAuth({
        baseURL: env.BETTER_AUTH_URL,
        db,
        secret: env.BETTER_AUTH_SECRET,
      }),
      db,
    };

    const response = await handle(services);

    if (response.body === null) {
      return response;
    }

    const { readable, writable } = new TransformStream<Uint8Array>();
    const forwarded = new Response(readable, response);
    const completed = response.body.pipeTo(writable).finally(dispose);
    streaming = true;
    waitUntil(completed);

    return forwarded;
  } finally {
    if (!streaming) {
      await dispose();
    }
  }
};

#   spa-hono-postgres-expo-void
#   spa-hono-postgres-expo-void-e2e
#   spa-hono-postgres-void
#   spa-hono-postgres-void-e2e
import { env as bindings, waitUntil } from "cloudflare:workers";
import { env } from "void/env";

import { createDb } from "@my-app/db";
import type { Database } from "@my-app/db";

export interface Services {
  db: Database;
}

export const withServices = async (
  handle: (services: Services) => Promise<Response>
) => {
  const db = createDb(env.DATABASE_URL ?? bindings.HYPERDRIVE.connectionString);
  let streaming = false;

  const dispose = async () => {
    await db.$client.end();
  };

  try {
    const services = {
      db,
    };

    const response = await handle(services);

    if (response.body === null) {
      return response;
    }

    const { readable, writable } = new TransformStream<Uint8Array>();
    const forwarded = new Response(readable, response);
    const completed = response.body.pipeTo(writable).finally(dispose);
    streaming = true;
    waitUntil(completed);

    return forwarded;
  } finally {
    if (!streaming) {
      await dispose();
    }
  }
};

import { createEnv } from "@t3-oss/env-core";
import { z } from "zod";

export const env = createEnv({
  emptyStringAsUndefined: true,
  runtimeEnv: process.env,
  server: {
    BETTER_AUTH_SECRET: z.string().min(32),
    BETTER_AUTH_URL: z.url(),
    DATABASE_URL: z.url(),
    PORT: z.coerce.number().int().positive().default(3000),
  },
});

import { createEnv } from "@t3-oss/env-core";
import { z } from "zod";

export const env = createEnv({
  emptyStringAsUndefined: true,
  runtimeEnv: process.env,
  server: {
    BETTER_AUTH_SECRET: z.string().min(32),
    BETTER_AUTH_URL: z.url(),
    DATABASE_URL: z.url(),
    NODE_ENV: z.enum(["development", "production", "test"]).optional(),
    PORT: z.coerce.number().int().positive().default(3000),
  },
});

import { createEnv } from "@t3-oss/env-core";
import { z } from "zod";

export const env = createEnv({
  emptyStringAsUndefined: true,
  runtimeEnv: process.env,
  server: {
    BETTER_AUTH_SECRET: z.string().min(32),
    BETTER_AUTH_URL: z.url(),
    DATABASE_URL: z.url(),
    NODE_ENV: z.enum(["development", "production", "test"]).optional(),
    PORT: z.coerce.number().int().positive().default(3001),
  },
});

import { once } from "node:events";

import { serve } from "@hono/node-server";

import { app } from "#src/app.ts";
import { db } from "#src/context.ts";
import { env } from "#src/env.ts";

const server = serve({ fetch: app.fetch, port: env.PORT }, ({ port }) => {
  console.log(`Server listening on http://localhost:${port}`);
});

await Promise.race([once(process, "SIGINT"), once(process, "SIGTERM")]);

server.close();

await once(server, "close");

await db.$client.promise().end();

import { once } from "node:events";
import { existsSync } from "node:fs";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

import { serveStatic } from "@hono/bun";
import { serve } from "bun";

import { app } from "#src/app.ts";
import { env } from "#src/env.ts";

const publicDir = fileURLToPath(new URL("public", import.meta.url));

if (existsSync(publicDir)) {
  app
    .use(serveStatic({ root: publicDir }))
    .get("*", serveStatic({ root: publicDir, path: "index.html" }));
}

const server = serve({ fetch: app.fetch, port: env.PORT });
const { port } = server;

console.log(`Server listening on http://localhost:${port}`);

await Promise.race([once(process, "SIGINT"), once(process, "SIGTERM")]);

await server.stop();

import { serve } from "@hono/node-server";
import { serveStatic } from "@hono/node-server/serve-static";

import { app } from "#src/app.ts";
import { env } from "#src/env.ts";

const publicDir = fileURLToPath(new URL("public", import.meta.url));

if (existsSync(publicDir)) {
  app
    .use(serveStatic({ root: publicDir }))
    .get("*", serveStatic({ root: publicDir, path: "index.html" }));
}

const server = serve({ fetch: app.fetch, port: env.PORT }, ({ port }) => {
  console.log(`Server listening on http://localhost:${port}`);
});

await Promise.race([once(process, "SIGINT"), once(process, "SIGTERM")]);

server.close();

import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

import { serveStatic } from "@hono/bun";
import { serve } from "bun";

import { app } from "#src/app.ts";
import { db } from "#src/context.ts";
import { env } from "#src/env.ts";

const publicDir = fileURLToPath(new URL("public", import.meta.url));

if (existsSync(publicDir)) {
  app
    .use(serveStatic({ root: publicDir }))
    .get("*", serveStatic({ root: publicDir, path: "index.html" }));
}

const server = serve({ fetch: app.fetch, port: env.PORT });
const { port } = server;

console.log(`Server listening on http://localhost:${port}`);

await Promise.race([once(process, "SIGINT"), once(process, "SIGTERM")]);

await server.stop();

await db.connection.close();

import { once } from "node:events";
import { existsSync } from "node:fs";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

import { serve } from "@hono/node-server";
import { serveStatic } from "@hono/node-server/serve-static";

import { app } from "#src/app.ts";
import { db } from "#src/context.ts";
import { env } from "#src/env.ts";

const publicDir = fileURLToPath(new URL("public", import.meta.url));

if (existsSync(publicDir)) {
  app
    .use(serveStatic({ root: publicDir }))
    .get("*", serveStatic({ root: publicDir, path: "index.html" }));
}

const server = serve({ fetch: app.fetch, port: env.PORT }, ({ port }) => {
  console.log(`Server listening on http://localhost:${port}`);
});

await Promise.race([once(process, "SIGINT"), once(process, "SIGTERM")]);

server.close();

await once(server, "close");

db.$client.close();

import { OpenAPIHandler } from "@orpc/openapi/fetch";
import { OpenAPIReferencePlugin } from "@orpc/openapi/plugins";
import { ORPCError, onError } from "@orpc/server";
import { RPCHandler } from "@orpc/server/fetch";
import { ZodToJsonSchemaConverter } from "@orpc/zod/zod4";
import type { Context as HonoContext } from "hono";

import { appRouter } from "@my-app/api";
import type { Context } from "@my-app/api";

const logServerError = (cause: unknown) => {
  if (!(cause instanceof ORPCError) || cause.status >= 500) {
    console.error(cause);
  }
};

const mount =
  (
    handler: RPCHandler<Context> | OpenAPIHandler<Context>,
    prefix: `/${string}`
  ) =>
  async (c: HonoContext) => {
    const { matched, response } = await handler.handle(c.req.raw, {
      context: {},
      prefix,
    });

    return matched
      ? c.newResponse(response.body, response)
      : await c.notFound();
  };

export const rpc = mount(
  new RPCHandler(appRouter, { interceptors: [onError(logServerError)] }),
  "/rpc"
);

export const openApi = mount(
  new OpenAPIHandler(appRouter, {
    interceptors: [onError(logServerError)],
    plugins: [
      new OpenAPIReferencePlugin({
        schemaConverters: [new ZodToJsonSchemaConverter()],
        specGenerateOptions: {
          info: {
            title: "my-app",
            version: "0.0.0",
          },
        },
      }),
    ],
  }),
  "/api"
);

import { OpenAPIHandler } from "@orpc/openapi/fetch";
import { OpenAPIReferencePlugin } from "@orpc/openapi/plugins";
import { ORPCError, onError } from "@orpc/server";
import { RPCHandler } from "@orpc/server/fetch";
import { ZodToJsonSchemaConverter } from "@orpc/zod/zod4";
import type { Context as HonoContext } from "hono";

import { appRouter } from "@my-app/api";
import type { Context } from "@my-app/api";

import { auth, db } from "#src/context.ts";

const logServerError = (cause: unknown) => {
  if (!(cause instanceof ORPCError) || cause.status >= 500) {
    console.error(cause);
  }
};

const mount =
  (
    handler: RPCHandler<Context> | OpenAPIHandler<Context>,
    prefix: `/${string}`
  ) =>
  async (c: HonoContext) => {
    const session = await auth.api.getSession({ headers: c.req.raw.headers });

    const { matched, response } = await handler.handle(c.req.raw, {
      context: { db, session },
      prefix,
    });

    return matched
      ? c.newResponse(response.body, response)
      : await c.notFound();
  };

export const rpc = mount(
  new RPCHandler(appRouter, { interceptors: [onError(logServerError)] }),
  "/rpc"
);

export const openApi = mount(
  new OpenAPIHandler(appRouter, {
    interceptors: [onError(logServerError)],
    plugins: [
      new OpenAPIReferencePlugin({
        schemaConverters: [new ZodToJsonSchemaConverter()],
        specGenerateOptions: {
          info: {
            title: "my-app",
            version: "0.0.0",
          },
        },
      }),
    ],
  }),
  "/api"
);

import { OpenAPIHandler } from "@orpc/openapi/fetch";
import { OpenAPIReferencePlugin } from "@orpc/openapi/plugins";
import { ORPCError, onError } from "@orpc/server";
import { RPCHandler } from "@orpc/server/fetch";
import { ZodToJsonSchemaConverter } from "@orpc/zod/zod4";
import type { Context as HonoContext } from "hono";

import { appRouter } from "@my-app/api";
import type { Context } from "@my-app/api";

import { db } from "#src/context.ts";

const logServerError = (cause: unknown) => {
  if (!(cause instanceof ORPCError) || cause.status >= 500) {
    console.error(cause);
  }
};

const mount =
  (
    handler: RPCHandler<Context> | OpenAPIHandler<Context>,
    prefix: `/${string}`
  ) =>
  async (c: HonoContext) => {
    const { matched, response } = await handler.handle(c.req.raw, {
      context: { db },
      prefix,
    });

    return matched
      ? c.newResponse(response.body, response)
      : await c.notFound();
  };

export const rpc = mount(
  new RPCHandler(appRouter, { interceptors: [onError(logServerError)] }),
  "/rpc"
);

export const openApi = mount(
  new OpenAPIHandler(appRouter, {
    interceptors: [onError(logServerError)],
    plugins: [
      new OpenAPIReferencePlugin({
        schemaConverters: [new ZodToJsonSchemaConverter()],
        specGenerateOptions: {
          info: {
            title: "my-app",
            version: "0.0.0",
          },
        },
      }),
    ],
  }),
  "/api"
);

#   spa-hono-orpc-void
#   spa-hono-orpc-void-e2e
import { OpenAPIHandler } from "@orpc/openapi/fetch";
import { OpenAPIReferencePlugin } from "@orpc/openapi/plugins";
import { ORPCError, onError } from "@orpc/server";
import { RPCHandler } from "@orpc/server/fetch";
import { ZodToJsonSchemaConverter } from "@orpc/zod/zod4";
import type { Context as HonoContext } from "hono";

import { appRouter } from "@my-app/api";
import type { Context } from "@my-app/api";

const logServerError = (cause: unknown) => {
  if (!(cause instanceof ORPCError) || cause.status >= 500) {
    console.error(cause);
  }
};

export const createRpc = () => {
  const mount =
    (
      handler: RPCHandler<Context> | OpenAPIHandler<Context>,
      prefix: `/${string}`
    ) =>
    async (c: HonoContext) => {
      const { matched, response } = await handler.handle(c.req.raw, {
        context: {},
        prefix,
      });

      return matched
        ? c.newResponse(response.body, response)
        : await c.notFound();
    };

  const rpc = mount(
    new RPCHandler(appRouter, { interceptors: [onError(logServerError)] }),
    "/rpc"
  );

  const openApi = mount(
    new OpenAPIHandler(appRouter, {
      interceptors: [onError(logServerError)],
      plugins: [
        new OpenAPIReferencePlugin({
          schemaConverters: [new ZodToJsonSchemaConverter()],
          specGenerateOptions: {
            info: {
              title: "my-app",
              version: "0.0.0",
            },
          },
        }),
      ],
    }),
    "/api"
  );

  return { rpc, openApi };
};

import { OpenAPIHandler } from "@orpc/openapi/fetch";
import { OpenAPIReferencePlugin } from "@orpc/openapi/plugins";
import { ORPCError, onError } from "@orpc/server";
import { RPCHandler } from "@orpc/server/fetch";
import { ZodToJsonSchemaConverter } from "@orpc/zod/zod4";
import type { Context as HonoContext } from "hono";

import { appRouter } from "@my-app/api";
import type { Context } from "@my-app/api";

import type { Services } from "#src/context.ts";

const logServerError = (cause: unknown) => {
  if (!(cause instanceof ORPCError) || cause.status >= 500) {
    console.error(cause);
  }
};

export const createRpc = ({ auth, db }: Services) => {
  const mount =
    (
      handler: RPCHandler<Context> | OpenAPIHandler<Context>,
      prefix: `/${string}`
    ) =>
    async (c: HonoContext) => {
      const session = await auth.api.getSession({ headers: c.req.raw.headers });

      const { matched, response } = await handler.handle(c.req.raw, {
        context: { db, session },
        prefix,
      });

      return matched
        ? c.newResponse(response.body, response)
        : await c.notFound();
    };

  const rpc = mount(
    new RPCHandler(appRouter, { interceptors: [onError(logServerError)] }),
    "/rpc"
  );

  const openApi = mount(
    new OpenAPIHandler(appRouter, {
      interceptors: [onError(logServerError)],
      plugins: [
        new OpenAPIReferencePlugin({
          schemaConverters: [new ZodToJsonSchemaConverter()],
          specGenerateOptions: {
            info: {
              title: "my-app",
              version: "0.0.0",
            },
          },
        }),
      ],
    }),
    "/api"
  );

  return { rpc, openApi };
};
